# SIGNAL-MATRIX (ov1104-detbench)

Columns: AUROC [95% cluster-bootstrap CI]; `R/F@1%`, `R/F@5%` = recall / FPR at the threshold that gives 1% / 5% FPR on pooled DEV negatives (abstain = not flagged); `fire R/F` = recall / FPR when a rule-like (<=3-valued) signal fires at its top value; cov = coverage; beats length = paired bootstrap of AUROC(signal) - AUROC(length_chars), yes/no/tie (diff). Held-out numbers are scored once with the DEV-frozen threshold. Decisive-rule sets are never pooled.
